[EVENT: TUR29302]
Date: 2023-04-01
Country: Turkey | Location: Aynali
Type: Violence against civilians > Attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Military Forces of Turkey (2016-) Gendarmerie [State forces]
Actor2: Civilians (Turkey) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 1
Sources: Human Rights Foundation of Turkey; Mezopotamya Agency [Other-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 April 2023, a civilian was shot and killed in Aynali village of Cermik, Diyarbakir by gendarmerie soldiers who raided his house to arrest him. The victim was unarmed but gendarmerie claimed he attempted to escape and failed to comply with their orders to stop.

---

[EVENT: TUR29256]
Date: 2023-04-01
Country: Turkey | Location: Sanliurfa
Type: Strategic developments > Change to group/activity | Disorder: Strategic developments
Actor1: Government of Turkey (2016-) [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Aydinlik; Cumhuriyet [National]

DESCRIPTION:
Security measures: On 1 April 2023, Sanliurfa Governor's office issued a ban on all forms of marches, protests, press conferences, sit-ins and other political gatherings in the province for 5 days in order to maintain public order and security. Similar measures have been imposed in the province (and several other southeastern provinces) for some years to maintain public order, presumably against political events that can be organized by Kurdish movements.

---

[EVENT: TUR29324]
Date: 2023-04-03
Country: Turkey | Location: Inegol
Type: Riots > Mob violence | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Rioters (Turkey) [Rioters]
Actor2: Civilians (Turkey) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Human Rights Foundation of Turkey; Rudaw [Other-Regional]

DESCRIPTION:
On 3 April 2023, a group of Bursaspor football club fans attacked a vehicle in Inegol district of Bursa, due to the number plate of the vehicle which is registered in the Kurdish-majority city of Diyarbakir. The attackers stopped the vehicle driving on a road and took his Kurdish driver out, assaulted him and also damaged the vehicle. The owner of the vehicle escaped the scene without his vehicle as the group continued vandalizing the vehicle.

[EVENT: TUR29449]

Country: Turkey | Location: Siirt
Type: Strategic developments > Change to group/activity | Disorder: Strategic developments
Actor1: Government of Turkey (2016-) [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Ihlas News Agency [National]

DESCRIPTION:
Security measures: On 15 April 2023, Siirt Governor's office issued a ban on all forms of marches, protests, press conferences, sit-ins and other political gatherings in the province for 15 days in order to maintain public order and security. Similar measures have been imposed in the province (and several other southeastern provinces) for some years to maintain public order, presumably against political events that can be organized by Kurdish movements.

DESCRIPTION:

[EVENT: TUR29579]
Date: 2023-05-13
Country: Turkey | Location: Ankara
Type: Strategic developments > Change to group/activity | Disorder: Strategic developments
Actor1: Government of Turkey (2016-) [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Bianet; Kurdistan24 [National-Regional]

DESCRIPTION:
Security measures: On 13 May 2023, the Supreme Election Board of Turkey issued a number of nationwide election measures as per the election law. As part of the announced measures, as of 6 p.m. on May 13, a 24-hour propaganda ban is enforced across Turkey until 6 p.m on 14 May, prohibiting all news and broadcasts related to the election and election results. Also on the election day (14 May), from 6 a.m. until midnight, the sale of alcoholic beverages is prohibited, all public entertainment places remain closed and citizens are not allowed to carry guns except the state's security forces. Coded to capital city (Ankara).

DESCRIPTION:

Actor1: Protesters (Turkey) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Sozcu [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 4 June 2023, various women's rights groups, including We Will Stop Femicides Movement members, staged a demonstration in Tepebasi town, Eskisehir, protesting a new project that will allow imams to be instructors in public schools. The protesters demanded scientific and secular education for young girls. A CHP MP also attended the event.

[EVENT: TUR29954]
Date: 2023-06-04
Country: Turkey | Location: Cizre
Type: Violence against civilians > Attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: PKK: Kurdistan Workers Party [Rebel group]
Actor2: Civilians (Turkey) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 1
Sources: ANF News; Kurdistan24; Rudaw; Sozcu; Takvim [National-Regional]

DESCRIPTION:
On 4 June 2023, PKK militants attacked an off-duty village guard who was in his personal car in Sur neighborhood of Cizre town, Sirnak. The militants opened fire on vehicle using their weapons, then set the vehicle on fire with the critically wounded victim inside, who later succumbed to his injuries. PKK claimed the responsibility of the attack, stating that the victim was 'punished' for the role his plays in the security operations carried out in the region.
